## 1. Architecture Overview & Global Standards

### 1.1 Base URLs & Environments
* **Production**: `https://api.amara-app.com/api/v1`
* **Staging / QA**: `https://staging-api.amara-app.com/api/v1`
* **Local Development**: `http://localhost:9395` or LAN IP `http://192.168.1.x:9395`
* **Client Protocol**: RESTful JSON over HTTPS (with Multipart for image uploads).

### 1.2 Authentication & Authorization
* **Mechanism**: HTTP Bearer Token (JWT).
* **Header Format**:
  ```http
  Authorization: Bearer <JWT_ACCESS_TOKEN>
  ```
* All endpoints require an active Bearer Token unless explicitly marked as **Public** in this document.
* **Token Expiration**: Recommended 30 days for mobile devices (with optional refresh token rotation).
* The Axios client automatically attaches this token to every non-public request.

### 1.3 Standard Response Formats

#### Success Envelope (HTTP 200 OK / 201 Created)
```json
{
  "status": "success",
  "message": "Operation completed successfully",
  "data": { ... }
}
```
*(Note: Some entity endpoints return direct objects or arrays as documented per endpoint below).*

#### Error Envelope (HTTP 400, 401, 403, 404, 409, 422, 500)
```json
{
  "status": "error",
  "message": "Human-readable error description for client display",
  "code": "ERROR_CODE_STRING",
  "details": null
}
```

### 1.4 User Identifier Conventions
* Primary User Identifiers are string tokens formatted as `usr_man_<id>` or `usr_woman_<id>` or UUIDv4 (e.g., `"usr_man_101"`, `"usr_woman_201"`).
* Both numeric `id` and alphanumeric `userId` must be consistently supported by the backend.

## 2. Domain 1: Authentication & Account Lifecycle

### 💡 Development Environment OTP Note
* For local development, QA testing, and automated tests, the backend should accept the static test code **`123456`** for `POST /verify-register/otp` and `POST /forgot-password/reset`.
* In production (`NODE_ENV === 'production'`), real email/SMS dispatch should be executed, and the OTP must not be exposed in client-facing logs.

### 💡 OTP Delivery & Backend SMTP Architecture

| Requirement / Component | Needed for OTPs? | Notes |
| :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **Backend SMTP Configuration** | ✅ **Yes** | The backend simply needs standard Gmail SMTP credentials to send the verification email. |
| **Push Notifications (FCM / APNs)** | ❌ **No** | Push notifications are not used for sending OTPs. |
| **In-App Notification Preferences** | ❌ **No** | User notification settings only apply to date and match alerts. OTPs are mandatory security verifications and must always be delivered. |
| **Client Auto-Read / SMS Retriever** | ❌ **No** | Mobile operating systems sandbox email; users enter the 6-digit code manually or via clipboard paste. |

#### Backend SMTP Configuration Details (Gmail SMTP)
To enable real email dispatch for registration OTPs (`POST /register`), password reset codes (`POST /forgot-password/send-otp`), and notification digests:
* **Host**: `smtp.gmail.com`
* **Port**: `587` (STARTTLS) or `465` (SSL)
* **Auth**: Standard Google App Password (generated via Google Account > Security > 2-Step Verification > App passwords)
* **Environment Variables**:
  ```env
  SMTP_HOST=smtp.gmail.com
  SMTP_PORT=587
  SMTP_SECURE=false
  SMTP_USER=your-amara-service-account@gmail.com
  SMTP_PASS=your-16-char-google-app-password
  SMTP_FROM="AMARA Dating <no-reply@amara-app.com>"
  ```

---

### 2.1 Initiate Registration
* **Endpoint**: `POST /register` (or `POST /auth/register`)
* **Access**: Public
* **Description**: Initiates registration with name, email (or mobile), password, and confirmPassword. Dispatches an OTP verification code.
* **Request Body**:
  ```json
  {
    "name": "Rahul Sharma",
    "email": "rahul@amara.com",
    "password": "Password@123",
    "confirmPassword": "Password@123",
    "mobile": "9876543210"
  }
  ```
* **Success Response (200 OK)**:
  ```json
  {
    "status": "success",
    "message": "Registration initiated. Verification code sent to rahul@amara.com.",
    "sessionId": "sess_reg_1728901234",
    "data": {
      "sessionId": "sess_reg_1728901234",
      "email": "rahul@amara.com",
      "name": "Rahul Sharma"
    }
  }
  ```
* **Error Responses**:
  - `400 Bad Request`: `{ "status": "error", "message": "Please enter a valid email address." }`
  - `400 Bad Request`: `{ "status": "error", "message": "Passwords do not match." }`
  - `400 Bad Request`: `{ "status": "error", "message": "Password must be at least 6 characters." }`
  - `409 Conflict`: `{ "status": "error", "message": "An account with this email already exists." }`

---

### 2.2 Verify Registration OTP & Complete Account Creation
* **Endpoint**: `POST /verify-register/otp` (or `POST /verifyOtp`)
* **Access**: Public
* **Description**: Verifies the OTP, creates the user record, and issues a JWT token.
* **Request Body**:
  ```json
  {
    "email": "rahul@amara.com",
    "otp": "123456",
    "sessionId": "sess_reg_1728901234",
    "name": "Rahul Sharma",
    "password": "Password@123"
  }
  ```
* **Success Response (200 OK)**:
  ```json
  {
    "status": "success",
    "message": "User registered successfully",
    "token": "eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9...",
    "userId": "usr_man_101",
    "user": {
      "id": 1,
      "userId": "usr_man_101",
      "name": "Rahul Sharma",
      "displayName": "Rahul Sharma",
      "email": "rahul@amara.com",
      "mobile": "9876543210",
      "gender": "man",
      "telegramUsername": "",
      "profile": null
    }
  }
  ```
* **Error Response (400 Bad Request)**:
  ```json
  {
    "status": "error",
    "message": "Invalid OTP. Please check and try again."
  }
  ```

---

### 2.3 User Login
* **Endpoint**: `POST /login` (or `POST /auth/login`)
* **Access**: Public
* **Description**: Authenticates user using email (or mobile) and password. Returns JWT token and user profile.
* **Request Body**:
  ```json
  {
    "email": "rahul@amara.com",
    "password": "Password@123"
  }
  ```
* **Success Response (200 OK)**:
  ```json
  {
    "token": "eyJhbGciOiJIUzI1NiIsInR5cCI6IkpXVCJ9...",
    "userId": "usr_man_101",
    "user": {
      "id": 1,
      "userId": "usr_man_101",
      "name": "Rahul Sharma",
      "displayName": "Rahul Sharma",
      "email": "rahul@amara.com",
      "mobile": "9876543210",
      "gender": "man",
      "telegramUsername": "rahul_amara",
      "profile": {
        "age": 28,
        "bio": "Entrepreneur and travel enthusiast.",
        "photos": ["https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1500648767791-00dcc994a43e?w=800"],
        "photoVerified": true
      }
    }
  }
  ```
* **Error Responses**:
  - `400 Bad Request`: `{ "status": "error", "message": "Email and password are required." }`
  - `401 Unauthorized`: `{ "status": "error", "message": "Invalid credentials. Please verify your password." }`
  - `404 Not Found`: `{ "status": "error", "message": "Account not found. Please register first." }`

---

### 2.4 Forgot Password — Send OTP
* **Endpoint**: `POST /forgot-password/send-otp`
* **Access**: Public
* **Request Body**:
  ```json
  {
    "email": "rahul@amara.com"
  }
  ```
* **Success Response (200 OK)**:
  ```json
  {
    "status": "success",
    "message": "Password reset OTP sent successfully to rahul@amara.com",
    "sessionId": "reset_sess_998124"
  }
  ```

---

### 2.5 Forgot Password — Reset Password
* **Endpoint**: `POST /forgot-password/reset`
* **Access**: Public
* **Request Body**:
  ```json
  {
    "email": "rahul@amara.com",
    "otp": "123456",
    "sessionId": "reset_sess_998124",
    "newPassword": "NewPassword@123",
    "confirmPassword": "NewPassword@123"
  }
  ```
* **Success Response (200 OK)**:
  ```json
  {
    "status": "success",
    "message": "Password reset successfully. You can now login with your new password."
  }
  ```

---

### 2.6 Account Lifecycle (Deactivate, Activate, Delete)
* **Deactivate Account**: `POST /account/deactivate` (Bearer Auth)
  - Query/Body: `{ userId?: string }`
  - Response: `{ "status": "success", "message": "Account deactivated successfully." }`
* **Reactivate Account**: `PUT /account/activate` (Bearer Auth)
  - Query/Body: `{ userId?: string }`
  - Response: `{ "status": "success", "message": "Account reactivated successfully." }`
* **Permanently Delete Account**: `DELETE /account/delete` (Bearer Auth)
  - Query/Body: `{ userId?: string }`
  - Action: Permanently purges user record, images, subscriptions, matches, and location data in compliance with GDPR.
  - Response: `{ "status": "success", "message": "Account permanently deleted." }`

## 3. Domain 2: Onboarding & Profile Setup (Steps 1 to 8)

The frontend guides newly registered users through an 8-step setup sequence:

### 3.1 Step 1: Gender & Orientation
* **Endpoint**: `POST /profile/gender-orientation`
* **Access**: Bearer Auth
* **Request Body**:
  ```json
  {
    "userId": "usr_man_101",
    "gender": "man",
    "orientation": "straight"
  }
  ```
  *(Allowed values: gender: `"man"`, `"woman"`, `"lgbtqia"`; orientation: `"straight"`, `"lgbtqia"`)*
* **Success Response (200 OK)**:
  ```json
  {
    "status": "success",
    "message": "Gender and orientation saved successfully",
    "data": {
      "userId": "usr_man_101",
      "gender": "man",
      "orientation": "straight"
    }
  }
  ```

### 3.2 Step 2: Display Name & Step 3: Date of Birth
* User provides their preferred display name and birthday.
* App enforces that the user must be at least 18 years old:
  - If underage: Rejects registration with `"You must be at least 18 years old to use AMARA."`
* Values are committed in final profile setup.

### 3.3 Step 4: Biometric Selfie Verification
* **Endpoint**: `PUT /profile/selfie/verify/:userId` (or `POST /profile/selfie/upload`)
* **Access**: Bearer Auth
* **Description**: User performs a live 3D head scan via camera. Upon completion, marks `photoVerified: true` on their profile to display the blue verified badge.
* **Success Response (200 OK)**:
  ```json
  {
    "status": "success",
    "message": "Verified",
    "data": {
      "userId": "usr_man_101",
      "photoVerified": true
    }
  }
  ```

### 3.4 Step 5: Multi-Photo Upload (3 to 6 Photos)
* **Upload Image**: `POST /profile/upload-image`
  - **Headers**: `Content-Type: multipart/form-data`
  - **Form Field**: `photo` or `image` (binary JPEG/PNG image)
  - **Query/Param**: `uid?: string`
  - **Success Response (200 OK)**:
    ```json
    {
      "status": "success",
      "message": "Image uploaded successfully",
      "imageUrl": "https://cdn.amara-app.com/photos/usr_man_101_1.jpg",
      "data": {
        "id": 101,
        "imageUrl": "https://cdn.amara-app.com/photos/usr_man_101_1.jpg",
        "isProfile": true
      }
    }
    ```
* **Get User Gallery Images**: `GET /users/:userId/images`
  - **Success Response (200 OK)**:
    ```json
    [
      { "id": 101, "userId": "usr_man_101", "imageUrl": "https://cdn.amara-app.com/photos/1.jpg", "isProfile": true },
      { "id": 102, "userId": "usr_man_101", "imageUrl": "https://cdn.amara-app.com/photos/2.jpg", "isProfile": false },
      { "id": 103, "userId": "usr_man_101", "imageUrl": "https://cdn.amara-app.com/photos/3.jpg", "isProfile": false }
    ]
    ```
* **Set Primary Profile Avatar**: `PUT /users/:userId/profile-photo/:imageId`
  - **Success Response (200 OK)**: `{ "status": "success", "message": "Profile photo set successfully" }`
* **Delete Photo**: `DELETE /users/images/:imageId` (or `DELETE /users/:userId/images/:imageId`)
  - **Success Response (200 OK)**: `{ "status": "success", "message": "Image deleted successfully" }`

### 3.5 Step 6: Connect Telegram
* **Endpoint**: `POST /telegram/connect`
* **Request Body / Query**: `{ "userId": "usr_man_101", "username": "rahul_amara" }`
* **Success Response (200 OK)**:
  ```json
  {
    "status": "success",
    "message": "Telegram account connected successfully",
    "telegramUsername": "rahul_amara",
    "telegramLink": "https://t.me/rahul_amara"
  }
  ```

### 3.6 Step 7: About Me & Step 8: Extended Lifestyle Setup
* **Endpoint**: `POST /profile/:userId/setup`
* **Request Body**:
  ```json
  {
    "name": "Rahul Sharma",
    "displayName": "Rahul",
    "gender": "man",
    "orientation": "straight",
    "age": 28,
    "dob": "1998-05-15",
    "bio": "Entrepreneur and travel enthusiast passionate about art, tech, and fitness.",
    "height": 178,
    "appearance": "Attractive",
    "bodyType": "Athletic",
    "language": "English, Spanish",
    "englishLevel": "Good",
    "ethnicity": "South Asian",
    "kidCount": "Someday",
    "netWorth": "$250k+",
    "lookingFor": "Relationship, Casual Dating",
    "smoke": "No",
    "drink": "Socially",
    "photos": [
      "https://cdn.amara-app.com/photos/usr_man_101_1.jpg",
      "https://cdn.amara-app.com/photos/usr_man_101_2.jpg",
      "https://cdn.amara-app.com/photos/usr_man_101_3.jpg"
    ]
  }
  ```
* **Success Response (200 OK)**:
  ```json
  {
    "status": "success",
    "message": "Profile setup completed successfully"
  }
  ```

### 3.7 Profile Completion Calculation
* **Endpoint**: `POST /profile/completion` or `GET /profile/completion`
* **Query**: `?userId=usr_man_101`
* **Success Response (200 OK)**:
  ```json
  {
    "percentage": 100,
    "isComplete": true,
    "missingFields": []
  }
  ```

## 5. Domain 4: Discovery Feed & Search Settings

### 5.1 Main Feed Algorithm
* **Endpoint**: `GET /home/:userId`
* **Business Logic**:
  1. Filters prospective matches based on user's gender, orientation, and search settings.
  2. For heterosexual men: default feed shows women profiles.
  3. **Strict Block Filtering**: Must exclude all users present in the caller's `blocked_users` table.
  4. Returns curated card array with distance, bio, verification status, and gallery photos.
* **Success Response (200 OK)**:
  ```json
  [
    {
      "id": "usr_woman_201",
      "userId": "usr_woman_201",
      "name": "Ananya Roy",
      "displayName": "Ananya",
      "age": 24,
      "gender": "woman",
      "city": "Mumbai",
      "country": "India",
      "distance": "5 km away",
      "bio": "Architect & design lover.",
      "photoVerified": true,
      "isOnline": true,
      "lastActive": "5m ago",
      "photos": [
        "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1494790108377-be9c29b29330?w=800"
      ],
      "appearance": "Attractive",
      "bodyType": "Slim",
      "height": 165,
      "language": "English, Spanish",
      "lookingFor": "Long-term relationship",
      "smoke": "No",
      "drink": "No"
    }
  ]
  ```

### 5.2 Filtered Search
* **Endpoint**: `POST /search` (or `POST /users/filter`)
* **Request Body**:
  ```json
  {
    "ageRange": [21, 32],
    "distance": 50,
    "searchWorldwide": false,
    "showMe": ["women"],
    "lookingFor": ["Relationship"],
    "bodyType": "Slim",
    "height": [150, 175],
    "smoke": "No",
    "drink": "No"
  }
  ```
  *(Note: Men can select `showMe: ["women"]`, `["men"]`, or `["women", "men"]`)*

### 5.3 Recent & Online Feeds
* **Online Members**: `GET /dashboard/online`
* **Newest Members**: `GET /dashboard/recent`

## 6. Domain 5: Connections, Invites & Mutual Matches

### 6.1 Send Connection Invitation
* **Endpoint**: `POST /connections/send`
* **Quota Enforcement**:
  - Checks sender's active subscription tier in `subscriptions` table.
  - Free: 0 or restricted daily invites.
  - Silver: 10 invites / day.
  - Gold: 20 invites / day.
  - Elite: Unlimited invites.
  - If limit reached: Returns `403 Forbidden` with `code: "QUOTA_EXCEEDED"`.
* **Request Body**:
  ```json
  {
    "senderId": "usr_man_101",
    "receiverId": "usr_woman_201"
  }
  ```
* **Success Response (200 OK)**:
  ```json
  {
    "status": "success",
    "message": "Invitation sent successfully",
    "data": {
      "id": 501,
      "requestId": "req_501",
      "senderId": "usr_man_101",
      "receiverId": "usr_woman_201",
      "status": "PENDING",
      "createdAt": "2026-10-14T16:00:00Z"
    }
  }
  ```

### 6.2 Sent & Received Invites
* **Sent**: `GET /connections/sent?userId=usr_man_101`
  ```json
  [
    {
      "id": 501,
      "requestId": "req_501",
      "senderId": "usr_man_101",
      "receiverId": "usr_woman_201",
      "status": "PENDING",
      "createdAt": "2026-10-14T16:00:00Z",
      "receiverProfile": { "name": "Ananya Roy", "photos": ["https://..."] }
    }
  ]
  ```
* **Received**: `GET /connections/received?userId=usr_woman_201`
* **Accept Invitation**: `PUT /connections/accept`
  - Request Body: `{ "requestId": "req_501", "userId": "usr_woman_201" }`
  - Response:
    ```json
    {
      "status": "success",
      "message": "Connection accepted. You can now chat on Telegram!",
      "match": true,
      "telegramLink": "https://t.me/rahul_amara"
    }
    ```
* **Reject Invitation**: `PUT /connections/reject` `{ "requestId": "req_501" }`
* **Check Status**: `GET /connections/status?senderId=usr_man_101&receiverId=usr_woman_201`
  - Response: `{ "status": "PENDING", "requestId": "req_501", "canInvite": false }`
* **Mutual Matches List**: `GET /connections/list?userId=usr_man_101`
  - Returns array of accepted connections with unlocked Telegram links.
* **Restore Access**: `POST /connections/restore-access` `{ "requestId": "req_501" }`

---

## 7. Domain 6: Telegram Connect & Direct Chat Unlocking

The AMARA platform unlocks authentic 1-on-1 private messaging via Telegram upon mutual match:

### 7.1 Telegram Integration Architecture
* **Direct Match Chatting (User-to-User)**:
  - When two users match or an invitation is accepted, the frontend directly opens the matched user's personal Telegram handle via `tg://resolve?domain=<username>` (falling back to `https://t.me/<username>`).
  - **Does NOT require a bot** to facilitate the chat — it connects users directly in Telegram DMs.
* **Official Amara Telegram Bot (`@amara_dating_bot`)**:
  - Used during onboarding (Step 5: Connect Telegram) to verify identity, welcome users, provide login/help commands (`/start`, `/login`, `/help`, `/connect`), and dispatch match/invitation alerts.

---

### 7.2 API Endpoints
* **Connect Username**: `POST /telegram/connect`
  - Request: `{ "userId": "usr_man_101", "username": "rahul_amara" }`
  - Response (200 OK):
    ```json
    {
      "status": "success",
      "message": "Telegram account connected successfully",
      "telegramUsername": "rahul_amara",
      "telegramLink": "https://t.me/rahul_amara"
    }
    ```
* **Get Link**: `GET /telegram/link?userId=usr_man_101`
  - Response (200 OK):
    ```json
    {
      "status": "success",
      "telegramUsername": "rahul_amara",
      "telegramLink": "https://t.me/rahul_amara"
    }
    ```
* **Disconnect**: `DELETE /telegram/disconnect?userId=usr_man_101`
  - Response (200 OK):
    ```json
    {
      "status": "success",
      "message": "Telegram account disconnected successfully"
    }
    ```

---

### 7.3 Backend Developer Guide: Production Setup & 24/7 Hosting for `telegram-bot/`

> [!IMPORTANT]
> The mobile frontend connects to the Telegram Bot via `tg://resolve?domain=amara_dating_bot`. For this bot to actively respond to users when they install the APK and tap "Open Bot", the backend developer **must host and run `telegram-bot/bot.js` continuously (24/7)** on the production backend server.

#### Step 1: Create or Confirm the Production Bot with Telegram @BotFather
1. Open Telegram and search for **`@BotFather`**.
2. Run `/newbot` (or `/mybots` to manage an existing bot).
3. Set the Bot Name (e.g. `Amara Dating`) and Username (e.g. `amara_dating_bot`).
4. Copy the API Token provided by BotFather (format: `123456789:ABCdefGhIJKlmNoPQRsTUVwxyZ`).
5. *(Note: If you choose a different username, inform the frontend team to update `AMARA_BOT_USERNAME` in `src/utils/telegramHelper.ts`)*.

#### Step 2: Configure Environment Variables
Inside the `telegram-bot/` directory on your server, create or update `.env`:
```env
TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN="YOUR_REAL_BOT_TOKEN_FROM_BOTFATHER"
APP_LOGIN_URL="amara://login"
```

#### Step 3: Deploy & Run 24/7 on the Linux Backend Server using PM2
The bot is a standard Node.js daemon using the Telegraf framework. To ensure it runs 24/7 and automatically restarts if the server reboots:

```bash
# 1. Navigate to the bot directory
cd /path/to/amara/telegram-bot

# 2. Install production dependencies
npm install --production

# 3. Install PM2 process manager globally (if not already installed)
sudo npm install -g pm2

# 4. Start the bot daemon
pm2 start bot.js --name "amara-telegram-bot"

# 5. Save the PM2 process list
pm2 save

# 6. Configure PM2 to auto-start on server reboot
pm2 startup
```

#### Step 4: Verification & Logs
* To check bot status: `pm2 status amara-telegram-bot`
* To view live bot logs: `pm2 logs amara-telegram-bot`
* Open Telegram on your phone and send `/start` to `@amara_dating_bot`. The bot will immediately reply with the welcome card and action buttons.

#### Step 5: (Optional) Sending Automated Match Notifications via the Bot
When a user receives a new invitation or mutual match, the backend can trigger the bot to dispatch an instant push message to the user's Telegram:
```javascript
// Example backend notification dispatch:
const { Telegraf } = require('telegraf');
const bot = new Telegraf(process.env.TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN);

async function notifyUserOnMatch(telegramChatId, matchedUserName) {
  try {
    await bot.telegram.sendMessage(
      telegramChatId,
      `🎉 *It's a Match!*

You and *${matchedUserName}* liked each other on Amara! Open Amara or tap below to start chatting.`,
      { parse_mode: 'Markdown' }
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Failed to dispatch Telegram message:', error);
  }
}
```

## 8. Domain 7: Subscriptions & Payments (Razorpay)

### 8.1 Plan Hierarchy
| Plan Code | Price (INR) | Validity | Daily Invites | Key Features |
| :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- | :--- |
| **FREE** | ₹0 | Unlimited | 0 (or trial) | Browse discovery feed |
| **SILVER** | ₹1,499 | 30 Days | 10 / day | Unlocks Telegram chat, standard search |
| **GOLD** | ₹2,999 | 90 Days | 20 / day | Priority discovery, worldwide search, badge |

### 8.2 Create Razorpay Order
* **Endpoint**: `POST /razorpay/create-order`
* **Request**: `{ "plan": "GOLD", "userId": "usr_man_101" }`
* **Success Response (200 OK)**:
  ```json
  {
    "status": "success",
    "orderId": "order_RZP_102938",
    "amount": 299900,
    "currency": "INR",
    "key": "rzp_live_xxxxxxxxxxxx",
    "plan": "GOLD",
    "name": "Amara Gold Plan",
    "description": "Unlimited invitations & priority matching for 90 days"
  }
  ```

### 8.3 Verify Signature & Activate Subscription
* **Endpoint**: `POST /razorpay/verify`
* **Verification Algorithm**:
  ```python
  # Server-side signature check
  expected_signature = hmac.new(
      bytes(RAZORPAY_SECRET, 'utf-8'),
      bytes(order_id + "|" + payment_id, 'utf-8'),
      hashlib.sha256
  ).hexdigest()
  ```
* **Request Body**:
  ```json
  {
    "orderId": "order_RZP_102938",
    "paymentId": "pay_RZP_998877",
    "signature": "a1b2c3d4e5f6...",
    "plan": "GOLD",
    "userId": "usr_man_101"
  }
  ```
* **Success Response (200 OK)**:
  ```json
  {
    "status": "success",
    "message": "Payment verified and subscription activated successfully",
    "plan": "GOLD",
    "durationDays": 90,
    "expiresAt": "2027-01-12T16:00:00Z"
  }
  ```

### 8.4 Check Subscription Status & Days Remaining
* **Status**: `GET /subscriber/status?userId=usr_man_101`
  ```json
  {
    "isSubscriber": true,
    "plan": "GOLD",
    "status": "ACTIVE",
    "expiresAt": "2027-01-12T16:00:00Z",
    "daysRemaining": 90,
    "dailyRequestLimit": 20
  }
  ```
* **Days Remaining**: `GET /subscriber/remaining-days?userId=usr_man_101`
  ```json
  {
    "remainingDays": 90,
    "plan": "GOLD",
    "isActive": true
  }
  ```
* **Webhook**: `POST /razorpay/webhook` (handles `order.paid`, `payment.failed`, `subscription.cancelled`)

## 9. Domain 8: Geolocation & Multi-City Matching

* **Add New Location**: `POST /location/add`
  - Request: `{ "userId": "usr_man_101", "city": "Mumbai", "state": "Maharashtra", "country": "India", "latitude": 19.0760, "longitude": 72.8777 }`
  - Response: `{ "status": "success", "locationId": 10 }`
* **Switch Active Location**: `PUT /location/switch?userId=usr_man_101&locationId=10`
* **Location History**: `GET /location/history/:userId`
* **Current Location**: `GET /location/current/:userId`
* **Nearby Profiles by Radius**: `GET /location/nearby?userId=usr_man_101&radius=50`

---

## 10. Domain 9: Safety, User Reporting & Blocking

### 10.1 Block User
* **Endpoint**: `POST /reports/block` (or `POST /users/block`)
* **Immediate Effect**:
  - When User A blocks User B, User B is immediately removed from User A's discovery feed, search results, and chat inbox.
  - User A is also removed from User B's feeds.
* **Request Body**:
  ```json
  {
    "blockerUserId": "usr_man_101",
    "blockedUserId": "usr_woman_207"
  }
  ```
* **Success Response (200 OK)**:
  ```json
  {
    "status": "success",
    "message": "User has been blocked. They will no longer appear in your feeds or messages.",
    "blockedUserId": "usr_woman_207"
  }
  ```
* **List Blocked Users**: `GET /reports/blocked?userId=usr_man_101` (or `GET /users/blocked`)
* **Unblock User**: `POST /reports/unblock` (or `POST /users/unblock`)
  - Request: `{ "blockerUserId": "usr_man_101", "unblockedUserId": "usr_woman_207" }`
  - Response: `{ "status": "success", "message": "User unblocked successfully." }`

### 10.2 Report Profile
* **Endpoint**: `POST /reports/report`
* **Request Body**:
  ```json
  {
    "reporterUserId": "usr_man_101",
    "reportedUserId": "usr_woman_207",
    "reason": "SPAM_SCAM",
    "details": "Soliciting external money transfer links."
  }
  ```
  *(Allowed reasons: `"SPAM_SCAM"`, `"INAPPROPRIATE_PHOTOS"`, `"HARASSMENT"`, `"UNDERAGE"`, `"FAKE_PROFILE"`, `"OTHER"`)*
* **List Reports by User**: `GET /reports/my?userId=usr_man_101`
* **Moderator Queue**: `GET /reports/against` & `PUT /reports/resolve/:reportId` `{ "status": "RESOLVED" }`

## 14. Domain 13: PostgreSQL Production Database Schema DDL

The following relational DDL can be directly executed in PostgreSQL to create all necessary tables:

```sql
-- 1. USERS TABLE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id VARCHAR(64) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    email VARCHAR(255) UNIQUE,
    mobile VARCHAR(32) UNIQUE,
    password_hash VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    display_name VARCHAR(128),
    gender VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'man',
    orientation VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'straight',
    telegram_username VARCHAR(128),
    is_active BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 2. USER PROFILES TABLE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_profiles (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id VARCHAR(64) UNIQUE NOT NULL REFERENCES users(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    age INT,
    dob DATE,
    bio TEXT,
    height INT DEFAULT 165,
    appearance VARCHAR(64),
    body_type VARCHAR(64),
    language VARCHAR(255),
    english_level VARCHAR(64),
    ethnicity VARCHAR(64),
    kid_count VARCHAR(64),
    net_worth VARCHAR(64),
    looking_for VARCHAR(255),
    smoke VARCHAR(64),
    drink VARCHAR(64),
    photo_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    is_online BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    last_active TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 3. USER IMAGES TABLE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_images (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES users(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    image_url TEXT NOT NULL,
    is_profile BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
    slot_index INT DEFAULT 0,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 4. CONNECTIONS / INVITATIONS TABLE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS connections (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    request_id VARCHAR(64) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    sender_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES users(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    receiver_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES users(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING', -- PENDING, ACCEPTED, REJECTED, CANCELLED
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT unique_connection_pair UNIQUE(sender_id, receiver_id)
);

-- 5. BLOCKED USERS TABLE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS blocked_users (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    blocker_user_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES users(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    blocked_user_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES users(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT unique_block_pair UNIQUE(blocker_user_id, blocked_user_id)
);

-- 6. REPORTS TABLE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS reports (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    reporter_user_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES users(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    reported_user_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES users(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    reason VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
    details TEXT,
    status VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT 'OPEN', -- OPEN, RESOLVED, DISMISSED
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 7. SUBSCRIPTIONS TABLE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS subscriptions (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES users(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    plan VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL, -- FREE, SILVER, GOLD
    status VARCHAR(32) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'ACTIVE',
    razorpay_order_id VARCHAR(128),
    razorpay_payment_id VARCHAR(128),
    daily_request_limit INT DEFAULT 10,
    starts_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    expires_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 8. USER LOCATIONS TABLE
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS user_locations (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    user_id VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL REFERENCES users(user_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    city VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    state VARCHAR(128),
    country VARCHAR(128) NOT NULL,
    latitude DECIMAL(10, 8),
    longitude DECIMAL(11, 8),
    is_current BOOLEAN DEFAULT TRUE,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 9. APP TRANSLATIONS TABLE (For dynamic database-backed localization)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS app_translations (
    id SERIAL PRIMARY KEY,
    string_key VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    lang_code VARCHAR(10) NOT NULL, -- en, es, pt, de, fr, ro, ru, zh, ja, id
    translation_text TEXT NOT NULL,
    updated_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    CONSTRAINT unique_translation_entry UNIQUE(string_key, lang_code)
);

CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_trans_key_lang ON app_translations(string_key, lang_code);
```

---

## 15. Domain 14: Multi-Language Database Seed (10 Languages / 635 Keys Each)

The AMARA mobile client supports **10 international languages** with exactly 635 standardized dot-notated string keys per language.

### How to insert these translations into your Database:
1. **Automated SQL Script Execution**:
   Run the generated seed script directly against your database:
   ```bash
   psql -U your_db_user -d amara_db -f scripts/seed_i18n.sql
   ```
2. **Method A (Relational Key-Value Table)**:
   Use the `app_translations` table schema above:
   ```sql
   INSERT INTO app_translations (string_key, lang_code, translation_text)
   VALUES ('home.usercard.nearby', 'en', 'Nearby')
   ON CONFLICT (string_key, lang_code) DO UPDATE SET translation_text = EXCLUDED.translation_text;
   ```
3. **Method B (JSONB Document Table / MongoDB)**:
   Store each language pack as a single document/row:
   ```sql
   CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS language_packs (
       lang_code VARCHAR(10) PRIMARY KEY,
       name VARCHAR(64) NOT NULL,
       strings JSONB NOT NULL
   );
   ```
   And insert the JSON dictionary below directly into the `strings` column.

Below are the complete string dictionaries for all 10 supported languages ready for database seeding:

  "profile.howtousescreen.your_profile_is_completely_hidden_from_the_public_feed_women_cannot_se": "Your profile is completely hidden from the public feed. Women cannot search or see your pictures unless you choose to send them an invitation first.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.browse_verified_female_profiles_nearby_or_worldwide_find_someone_you_l": "Browse verified female profiles nearby or worldwide. Find someone you like and send a direct date invitation (membership required).",
  "profile.howtousescreen.when_the_woman_receives_your_invitation_she_reviews_your_profile_photo": "When the woman receives your invitation, she reviews your profile, photos, and date proposal. Only she can decide to accept or decline.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_endless_in_app_small_talk_once_she_accepts_both_of_your_telegram_ha": "No endless in-app small talk! Once she accepts, both of your Telegram handles are securely unlocked so you can chat and plan your date directly.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_is_completely_free_for_all_female_members_you_will_never_be_aske": "Amara is completely free for all female members. You will never be asked to pay for invitations, membership, or chatting.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.say_goodbye_to_hundreds_of_unwanted_messages_men_cannot_message_you_di": "Say goodbye to hundreds of unwanted messages. Men cannot message you directly; they can only send a formal date invitation for you to review.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.invitations_arrive_in_your_requests_inbox_review_their_verified_profil": "Invitations arrive in your Requests Inbox. Review their verified profile, photos, and background. You decide who you want to meet.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.when_you_accept_an_invite_your_telegram_handle_is_shared_with_your_mat": "When you accept an invite, your Telegram handle is shared with your match so you can chat conveniently where you already spend your time.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.why_are_mens_profiles_hidden_by_default": "Why are men’s profiles hidden by default?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.why_do_matches_move_to_telegram": "Why do matches move to Telegram?",

  "profile.howtousescreen.what_is_our_policy_on_sugar_dating_escorting": "What is our policy on sugar dating & escorting?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_follows_a_private_luxury_model_many_successful_ambitious_men_val": "Amara follows a private luxury model. Many successful, ambitious men value discretion. By hiding male profiles from the public feed, your identity remains private until you choose to invite someone.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.we_believe_dating_should_lead_to_real_life_meetings_not_endless_in_app": "We believe dating should lead to real-life meetings, not endless in-app texting. Once mutual interest is confirmed, exchanging Telegram handles allows convenient, encrypted, and direct communication.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.every_member_must_complete_a_real_time_selfie_verification_during_onbo": "Every member must complete a real-time selfie verification during onboarding. This guarantees that every profile belongs to an authentic, live human and eliminates catfishing.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_strictly_prohibits_compensated_dating_sugar_arrangements_prostit": "Amara strictly prohibits compensated dating, sugar arrangements, prostitution, or any financial exchange between members. Accounts violating these standards are banned permanently.",

  "profile.howtousescreen.how_amara_works": "Cómo funciona Amara",
  "profile.howtousescreen.exclusive_dating_experience": "EXPERIENCIA DE CITAS EXCLUSIVA",
  "profile.howtousescreen.dating_built_for_real_life": "Citas pensadas para la vida real.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_endless_swiping_no_spam_just_verified_singles_connecting_for_real_d": "Sin deslizamientos infinitos. Sin spam. Solo personas verificadas que conectan para citas reales.",

  "profile.howtousescreen.every_profile_completes_a_live_gesture_selfie": "Cada perfil completa un selfie en vivo con gesto.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_commercial_dating": "Sin citas comerciales:",
  "profile.howtousescreen.escorts_prostitution_and_sugar_dating_are_strictly_prohibited": "El escort, la prostitución y el sugar dating están estrictamente prohibidos.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.high_quality": "Alta calidad:",
  "profile.howtousescreen.profiles_with_low_photo_quality_or_abusive_behavior_are_removed": "Los perfiles con fotos de baja calidad o comportamiento abusivo se eliminan.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.frequently_asked_questions": "Preguntas frecuentes",
  "profile.howtousescreen.common_questions_about_using_the_amara_dating_platform": "Preguntas comunes sobre el uso de la plataforma de citas Amara.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.100_private_profile": "Perfil 100% privado",
  "profile.howtousescreen.send_date_invitations": "Envía invitaciones para citas",
  "profile.howtousescreen.review_acceptance": "Revisión y aceptación",
  "profile.howtousescreen.connect_on_telegram": "Conecta por Telegram",
  "profile.howtousescreen.100_free_for_women": "100% gratis para mujeres",
  "profile.howtousescreen.zero_spam_or_creepy_dms": "Cero spam ni mensajes raros",
  "profile.howtousescreen.you_are_always_in_control": "Tú siempre tienes el control",
  "profile.howtousescreen.chat_directly_on_telegram": "Chatea directamente por Telegram",
  "profile.howtousescreen.your_profile_is_completely_hidden_from_the_public_feed_women_cannot_se": "Tu perfil está completamente oculto del feed público. Las mujeres no pueden buscar ni ver tus fotos salvo que decidas enviarles primero una invitación.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.browse_verified_female_profiles_nearby_or_worldwide_find_someone_you_l": "Explora perfiles femeninos verificados cerca de ti o en todo el mundo. Encuentra a alguien que te guste y envíale una invitación directa para una cita (requiere membresía).",
  "profile.howtousescreen.when_the_woman_receives_your_invitation_she_reviews_your_profile_photo": "Cuando la mujer recibe tu invitación, revisa tu perfil, tus fotos y tu propuesta de cita. Solo ella puede decidir aceptar o rechazar.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_endless_in_app_small_talk_once_she_accepts_both_of_your_telegram_ha": "¡Sin conversaciones interminables en la app! Cuando acepte, los usuarios de Telegram de ambos se desbloquean de forma segura para que puedan chatear y planear la cita directamente.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_is_completely_free_for_all_female_members_you_will_never_be_aske": "Amara es totalmente gratuito para todas las miembros. Nunca se te pedirá pagar por invitaciones, membresía o chat.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.say_goodbye_to_hundreds_of_unwanted_messages_men_cannot_message_you_di": "Olvídate de cientos de mensajes no deseados. Los hombres no pueden escribirte directamente; solo pueden enviarte una invitación formal para una cita y tú la revisas.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.invitations_arrive_in_your_requests_inbox_review_their_verified_profil": "Las invitaciones llegan a tu bandeja de solicitudes. Revisa su perfil verificado, fotos y antecedentes. Tú decides con quién quieres quedar.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.when_you_accept_an_invite_your_telegram_handle_is_shared_with_your_mat": "Cuando aceptas una invitación, tu usuario de Telegram se comparte con tu match para que chateéis cómodamente donde ya pasáis el tiempo.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.why_are_mens_profiles_hidden_by_default": "¿Por qué los perfiles de hombres están ocultos por defecto?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.why_do_matches_move_to_telegram": "¿Por qué los matches pasan a Telegram?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.how_does_selfie_verification_work": "¿Cómo funciona la verificación con selfie?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.what_is_our_policy_on_sugar_dating_escorting": "¿Cuál es nuestra política sobre el sugar dating y el escort?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_follows_a_private_luxury_model_many_successful_ambitious_men_val": "Amara sigue un modelo de lujo privado. Muchos hombres exitosos y ambiciosos valoran la discreción. Al ocultar los perfiles masculinos del feed público, tu identidad permanece privada hasta que decidas invitar a alguien.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.we_believe_dating_should_lead_to_real_life_meetings_not_endless_in_app": "Creemos que las citas deben desembocar en encuentros reales, no en interminables mensajes dentro de la app. Cuando se confirma el interés mutuo, intercambiar usuarios de Telegram permite una comunicación cómoda, cifrada y directa.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.every_member_must_complete_a_real_time_selfie_verification_during_onbo": "Cada miembro debe completar una verificación con selfie en tiempo real durante el registro. Esto garantiza que cada perfil pertenezca a una persona real y auténtica, y elimina el catfishing.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_strictly_prohibits_compensated_dating_sugar_arrangements_prostit": "Amara prohíbe estrictamente las citas a cambio de dinero, los acuerdos de sugar, la prostitución y cualquier intercambio financiero entre miembros. Las cuentas que incumplan estas normas serán bloqueadas de forma permanente.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.privacy_first": "PRIVADO PRIMERO",

  "profile.howtousescreen.exclusive_dating_experience": "EXPERIÊNCIA DE NAMORO EXCLUSIVA",
  "profile.howtousescreen.dating_built_for_real_life": "Namoro feito para a vida real.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_endless_swiping_no_spam_just_verified_singles_connecting_for_real_d": "Sem swipes infinitos. Sem spam. Apenas solteiros verificados para encontros de verdade.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.for_men": "Para homens",

  "profile.howtousescreen.our_community_standards": "Nossos padrões da comunidade",
  "profile.howtousescreen.photo_verified": "Foto verificada:",
  "profile.howtousescreen.every_profile_completes_a_live_gesture_selfie": "Todo perfil realiza um selfie ao vivo com um gesto.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_commercial_dating": "Sem namoro comercial:",
  "profile.howtousescreen.escorts_prostitution_and_sugar_dating_are_strictly_prohibited": "Acompanhamento, prostituição e relacionamentos sugar são estritamente proibidos.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.high_quality": "Alta qualidade:",
  "profile.howtousescreen.profiles_with_low_photo_quality_or_abusive_behavior_are_removed": "Perfis com fotos de baixa qualidade ou comportamento abusivo são removidos.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.frequently_asked_questions": "Perguntas frequentes",
  "profile.howtousescreen.common_questions_about_using_the_amara_dating_platform": "Perguntas comuns sobre o uso da plataforma de namoro Amara.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.100_private_profile": "Perfil 100% privado",
  "profile.howtousescreen.send_date_invitations": "Envie convites para encontros",
  "profile.howtousescreen.review_acceptance": "Análise e aceitação",
  "profile.howtousescreen.connect_on_telegram": "Conecte no Telegram",
  "profile.howtousescreen.100_free_for_women": "100% grátis para mulheres",
  "profile.howtousescreen.zero_spam_or_creepy_dms": "Zero spam ou mensagens estranhas",
  "profile.howtousescreen.you_are_always_in_control": "Você sempre tem o controle",
  "profile.howtousescreen.chat_directly_on_telegram": "Converse direto no Telegram",
  "profile.howtousescreen.your_profile_is_completely_hidden_from_the_public_feed_women_cannot_se": "Seu perfil fica completamente oculto do feed público. Mulheres não podem buscar nem ver suas fotos, a menos que você escolha enviar um convite primeiro.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.browse_verified_female_profiles_nearby_or_worldwide_find_someone_you_l": "Navegue por perfis femininos verificados próximos ou no mundo todo. Encontre alguém que goste e envie um convite direto para um encontro (assinatura necessária).",
  "profile.howtousescreen.when_the_woman_receives_your_invitation_she_reviews_your_profile_photo": "Quando a mulher recebe seu convite, ela analisa seu perfil, fotos e proposta de encontro. Somente ela decide aceitar ou recusar.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_endless_in_app_small_talk_once_she_accepts_both_of_your_telegram_ha": "Sem conversas intermináveis dentro do app! Quando ela aceitar, os nomes de usuário do Telegram de vocês são desbloqueados com segurança para conversarem e planejarem o encontro direto.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_is_completely_free_for_all_female_members_you_will_never_be_aske": "O Amara é totalmente gratuito para todas as membros mulheres. Você nunca será cobrada por convites, assinatura ou conversas.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.say_goodbye_to_hundreds_of_unwanted_messages_men_cannot_message_you_di": "Diga adeus a centenas de mensagens indesejadas. Homens não podem te enviar mensagens diretamente; eles só podem enviar um convite formal de encontro para você analisar.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.invitations_arrive_in_your_requests_inbox_review_their_verified_profil": "Os convites chegam na sua caixa de solicitações. Analise o perfil verificado, as fotos e o histórico. Você decide com quem quer se encontrar.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.when_you_accept_an_invite_your_telegram_handle_is_shared_with_your_mat": "Quando você aceita um convite, seu nome de usuário do Telegram é compartilhado com seu match para que vocês conversem com praticidade onde já passam o tempo.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.why_are_mens_profiles_hidden_by_default": "Por que os perfis de homens ficam ocultos por padrão?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.why_do_matches_move_to_telegram": "Por que os matches vão para o Telegram?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.how_does_selfie_verification_work": "Como funciona a verificação por selfie?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.what_is_our_policy_on_sugar_dating_escorting": "Qual é a nossa política sobre namoro sugar e acompanhamento?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_follows_a_private_luxury_model_many_successful_ambitious_men_val": "O Amara segue um modelo privado de luxo. Muitos homens bem-sucedidos e ambiciosos valorizam a discrição. Ao ocultar os perfis masculinos do feed público, sua identidade permanece privada até você escolher convidar alguém.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.we_believe_dating_should_lead_to_real_life_meetings_not_endless_in_app": "Acreditamos que o namoro deve levar a encontros na vida real, e não a mensagens intermináveis no app. Quando o interesse mútuo é confirmado, trocar os nomes de usuário do Telegram permite uma comunicação prática, criptografada e direta.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.every_member_must_complete_a_real_time_selfie_verification_during_onbo": "Todo membro deve completar uma verificação de selfie em tempo real durante o cadastro. Isso garante que cada perfil pertença a uma pessoa autêntica e elimina perfis falsos.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_strictly_prohibits_compensated_dating_sugar_arrangements_prostit": "O Amara proíbe estritamente namoro remunerado, acordos sugar, prostituição ou qualquer troca financeira entre membros. Contas que violarem esses padrões são banidas permanentemente.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.privacy_first": "PRIVACIDADE PRIMEIRO",

  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.welcome_to_amara_we_respect_your_privacy_and_are_committed_to_protecti": "Bem-vindo ao Amara. Respeitamos sua privacidade e nos comprometemos a proteger seus dados pessoais. Esta política de privacidade explica como cuidamos dos seus dados pessoais quando você visita nosso aplicativo e informa sobre seus direitos de privacidade e como a lei os protege.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.personal_data_means_any_information_about_an_individual_from_which_tha": "Dados pessoais são quaisquer informações sobre um indivíduo pelas quais essa pessoa possa ser identificada. •",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.identity_data": "Dados de identidade:",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.includes_username_marital_status_title_date_of_birth_and_gender": "inclui nome de usuário, estado civil, título, data de nascimento e gênero. •",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.contact_data": "Dados de contato:",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.includes_email_address_and_telephone_numbers": "inclui endereço de e-mail e números de telefone. •",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.technical_data": "Dados técnicos:",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.includes_ip_address_login_data_device_info": "inclui endereço IP, dados de login e informações do dispositivo. •",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.profile_data": "Dados do perfil:",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.includes_interests_preferences_and_photos": "inclui interesses, preferências e fotos.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.we_use_your_data_mainly_to_provide_you_with_matching_partner_recommend": "Usamos seus dados principalmente para fornecer recomendações de parceiros compatíveis e para registrá-lo como novo usuário.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.we_retain_your_personal_data_only_as_long_as_necessary_to_fulfil_the_p": "Guardamos seus dados pessoais apenas pelo tempo necessário para cumprir as finalidades para as quais os coletamos.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.by_accessing_the_amara_application_you_are_agreeing_to_be_bound_by_the": "Ao acessar o aplicativo Amara, você concorda em estar sujeito a estes termos de serviço e a todas as leis aplicáveis.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.permission_is_granted_to_use_materials_on_amaras_app_for_personal_non": "É concedida permissão para usar os materiais do aplicativo Amara apenas para visualização pessoal, não comercial e temporária.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.always_exercise_caution_when_meeting_new_people_follow_our_safety_guid": "Sempre tenha cuidado ao conhecer novas pessoas. Siga nossas diretrizes de segurança fora do aplicativo.",

  "profile.howtousescreen.how_amara_works": "So funktioniert Amara",
  "profile.howtousescreen.exclusive_dating_experience": "EXKLUSIVES DATING-ERLEBNIS",
  "profile.howtousescreen.dating_built_for_real_life": "Dating fürs echte Leben.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_endless_swiping_no_spam_just_verified_singles_connecting_for_real_d": "Kein endloses Wischen. Kein Spam. Nur verifizierte Singles für echte Dates.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.for_men": "Für Männer",
  "profile.howtousescreen.for_women": "Für Frauen",
  "profile.howtousescreen.our_community_standards": "Unsere Community-Standards",
  "profile.howtousescreen.photo_verified": "Foto verifiziert:",
  "profile.howtousescreen.every_profile_completes_a_live_gesture_selfie": "Jedes Profil macht ein Live-Gesten-Selfie.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_commercial_dating": "Kein kommerzielles Dating:",
  "profile.howtousescreen.escorts_prostitution_and_sugar_dating_are_strictly_prohibited": "Escort, Prostitution und Sugar-Dating sind streng verboten.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.high_quality": "Hohe Qualität:",
  "profile.howtousescreen.profiles_with_low_photo_quality_or_abusive_behavior_are_removed": "Profile mit schlechter Fotoqualität oder missbräuchlichem Verhalten werden entfernt.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.frequently_asked_questions": "Häufig gestellte Fragen",
  "profile.howtousescreen.common_questions_about_using_the_amara_dating_platform": "Häufige Fragen zur Nutzung der Amara-Dating-Plattform.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.100_private_profile": "100 % privates Profil",
  "profile.howtousescreen.send_date_invitations": "Date-Einladungen senden",
  "profile.howtousescreen.review_acceptance": "Prüfung & Annahme",
  "profile.howtousescreen.connect_on_telegram": "Über Telegram verbinden",
  "profile.howtousescreen.100_free_for_women": "100 % kostenlos für Frauen",
  "profile.howtousescreen.zero_spam_or_creepy_dms": "Null Spam oder gruselige Direktnachrichten",
  "profile.howtousescreen.you_are_always_in_control": "Du hast immer die Kontrolle",
  "profile.howtousescreen.chat_directly_on_telegram": "Direkt auf Telegram chatten",
  "profile.howtousescreen.your_profile_is_completely_hidden_from_the_public_feed_women_cannot_se": "Dein Profil ist vollständig vor dem öffentlichen Feed verborgen. Frauen können dich nicht suchen oder deine Fotos sehen, wenn du nicht zuerst eine Einladung sendest.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.browse_verified_female_profiles_nearby_or_worldwide_find_someone_you_l": "Durchstöbere verifizierte weibliche Profile in der Nähe oder weltweit. Finde jemanden, der dir gefällt, und sende eine direkte Date-Einladung (Mitgliedschaft erforderlich).",
  "profile.howtousescreen.when_the_woman_receives_your_invitation_she_reviews_your_profile_photo": "Wenn die Frau deine Einladung erhält, prüft sie dein Profil, deine Fotos und deinen Date-Vorschlag. Nur sie entscheidet über Annahme oder Ablehnung.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_endless_in_app_small_talk_once_she_accepts_both_of_your_telegram_ha": "Kein endloses Smalltalk in der App! Wenn sie zustimmt, werden beide Telegram-Handles sicher entsperrt, damit ihr chatten und euren Date direkt planen könnt.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_is_completely_free_for_all_female_members_you_will_never_be_aske": "Amara ist für alle weiblichen Mitglieder völlig kostenlos. Du wirst nie gebeten, für Einladungen, Mitgliedschaft oder Chatten zu zahlen.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.say_goodbye_to_hundreds_of_unwanted_messages_men_cannot_message_you_di": "Verabschiede dich von hunderten unerwünschten Nachrichten. Männer können dir nicht direkt schreiben; sie können nur eine formelle Date-Einladung senden, die du prüfst.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.invitations_arrive_in_your_requests_inbox_review_their_verified_profil": "Einladungen kommen in dein Anfragen-Postfach. Prüfe ihr verifiziertes Profil, Fotos und Hintergrund. Du entscheidest, wen du treffen möchtest.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.when_you_accept_an_invite_your_telegram_handle_is_shared_with_your_mat": "Wenn du eine Einladung annimmst, wird dein Telegram-Handle mit deinem Match geteilt, damit ihr bequem dort chatten könnt, wo ihr euch sowieso aufhaltet.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.why_are_mens_profiles_hidden_by_default": "Warum sind Männerprofile standardmäßig verborgen?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.why_do_matches_move_to_telegram": "Warum wechseln Matches zu Telegram?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.how_does_selfie_verification_work": "Wie funktioniert die Selfie-Verifizierung?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.what_is_our_policy_on_sugar_dating_escorting": "Wie ist unsere Politik zu Sugar-Dating & Escort?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_follows_a_private_luxury_model_many_successful_ambitious_men_val": "Amara folgt einem privaten Luxusmodell. Viele erfolgreiche, ambitionierte Männer schätzen Diskretion. Durch das Verbergen männlicher Profile vor dem öffentlichen Feed bleibt deine Identität privat, bis du dich entscheidest, jemanden einzuladen.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.we_believe_dating_should_lead_to_real_life_meetings_not_endless_in_app": "Wir glauben, dass Dating zu Treffen im echten Leben führen sollte, nicht zu endlosem Schreiben in der App. Sobald das gegenseitige Interesse bestätigt ist, ermöglicht der Austausch der Telegram-Handles eine bequeme, verschlüsselte und direkte Kommunikation.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.every_member_must_complete_a_real_time_selfie_verification_during_onbo": "Jedes Mitglied muss während der Registrierung eine Echtzeit-Selfie-Verifizierung abschließen. Das garantiert, dass jedes Profil einem echten, lebenden Menschen gehört, und eliminiert Catfishing.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_strictly_prohibits_compensated_dating_sugar_arrangements_prostit": "Amara verbietet strikt bezahltes Dating, Sugar-Arrangements, Prostitution oder jeden finanziellen Austausch zwischen Mitgliedern. Konten, die diese Standards verletzen, werden dauerhaft gesperrt.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.privacy_first": "DATENSCHUTZ ZUERST",

  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.welcome_to_amara_we_respect_your_privacy_and_are_committed_to_protecti": "Willkommen bei Amara. Wir respektieren deine Privatsphäre und verpflichten uns, deine personenbezogenen Daten zu schützen. Diese Datenschutzerklärung informiert dich, wie wir deine Daten schützen, wenn du unsere App besuchst, und erklärt deine Datenschutzrechte sowie den gesetzlichen Schutz.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.personal_data_means_any_information_about_an_individual_from_which_tha": "Personenbezogene Daten sind alle Informationen über eine Person, anhand derer diese Person identifiziert werden kann. •",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.identity_data": "Identitätsdaten:",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.includes_username_marital_status_title_date_of_birth_and_gender": "umfasst Benutzername, Familienstand, Titel, Geburtsdatum und Geschlecht. •",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.contact_data": "Kontaktdaten:",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.includes_email_address_and_telephone_numbers": "umfasst E-Mail-Adresse und Telefonnummern. •",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.technical_data": "Technische Daten:",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.includes_ip_address_login_data_device_info": "umfasst IP-Adresse, Login-Daten, Geräteinformationen. •",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.profile_data": "Profildaten:",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.includes_interests_preferences_and_photos": "umfasst Interessen, Vorlieben und Fotos.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.we_use_your_data_mainly_to_provide_you_with_matching_partner_recommend": "Wir verwenden deine Daten hauptsächlich, um dir passende Partnerempfehlungen zu geben und dich als neuen Nutzer zu registrieren.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.we_retain_your_personal_data_only_as_long_as_necessary_to_fulfil_the_p": "Wir bewahren deine personenbezogenen Daten nur so lange auf, wie es zur Erfüllung der Zwecke erforderlich ist, für die wir sie erhoben haben.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.by_accessing_the_amara_application_you_are_agreeing_to_be_bound_by_the": "Mit dem Zugriff auf die Amara-App stimmst du diesen Nutzungsbedingungen und allen geltenden Gesetzen zu.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.permission_is_granted_to_use_materials_on_amaras_app_for_personal_non": "Die Nutzung der Materialien in der Amara-App ist nur zur persönlichen, nicht kommerziellen und vorübergehenden Betrachtung gestattet.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.always_exercise_caution_when_meeting_new_people_follow_our_safety_guid": "Sei stets vorsichtig, wenn du neue Menschen triffst. Befolge unsere Sicherheitsrichtlinien außerhalb der App.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.1_introduction": "1. Einleitung",

  "profile.howtousescreen.exclusive_dating_experience": "EXPÉRIENCE DE RENCONTRE EXCLUSIVE",
  "profile.howtousescreen.dating_built_for_real_life": "Des rencontres faites pour la vraie vie.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_endless_swiping_no_spam_just_verified_singles_connecting_for_real_d": "Pas de swipes infinis. Pas de spam. De simples célibataires vérifiés pour de vraies rencontres.",

  "profile.howtousescreen.escorts_prostitution_and_sugar_dating_are_strictly_prohibited": "L'escort, la prostitution et les relations sugar sont strictement interdits.",

  "profile.howtousescreen.profiles_with_low_photo_quality_or_abusive_behavior_are_removed": "Les profils de faible qualité ou au comportement abusif sont supprimés.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.frequently_asked_questions": "Foire aux questions",
  "profile.howtousescreen.common_questions_about_using_the_amara_dating_platform": "Questions fréquentes sur l'utilisation de la plateforme de rencontres Amara.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.100_private_profile": "Profil 100 % privé",
  "profile.howtousescreen.send_date_invitations": "Envoyer des invitations de rencontre",
  "profile.howtousescreen.review_acceptance": "Vérification & acceptation",
  "profile.howtousescreen.connect_on_telegram": "Se connecter sur Telegram",
  "profile.howtousescreen.100_free_for_women": "100 % gratuit pour les femmes",
  "profile.howtousescreen.zero_spam_or_creepy_dms": "Zéro spam ou messages gênants",
  "profile.howtousescreen.you_are_always_in_control": "Tu gardes toujours le contrôle",
  "profile.howtousescreen.chat_directly_on_telegram": "Discute directement sur Telegram",
  "profile.howtousescreen.your_profile_is_completely_hidden_from_the_public_feed_women_cannot_se": "Ton profil est complètement masqué du fil public. Les femmes ne peuvent ni te rechercher ni voir tes photos, sauf si tu choisis d'abord de leur envoyer une invitation.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.browse_verified_female_profiles_nearby_or_worldwide_find_someone_you_l": "Parcours les profils féminins vérifiés à proximité ou dans le monde entier. Trouve quelqu'un qui te plaît et envoie une invitation directe de rencontre (abonnement requis).",
  "profile.howtousescreen.when_the_woman_receives_your_invitation_she_reviews_your_profile_photo": "Quand la femme reçoit ton invitation, elle examine ton profil, tes photos et ta proposition de rencontre. Elle seule décide d'accepter ou de refuser.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_endless_in_app_small_talk_once_she_accepts_both_of_your_telegram_ha": "Pas de bavardages interminables dans l'app ! Dès qu'elle accepte, vos identifiants Telegram sont déverrouillés en toute sécurité pour discuter et organiser votre rencontre.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_is_completely_free_for_all_female_members_you_will_never_be_aske": "Amara est totalement gratuit pour toutes les femmes. On ne te demandera jamais de payer pour les invitations, l'abonnement ou les discussions.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.say_goodbye_to_hundreds_of_unwanted_messages_men_cannot_message_you_di": "Dis adieu à des centaines de messages non désirés. Les hommes ne peuvent pas t'écrire directement ; ils peuvent seulement envoyer une invitation formelle de rencontre à ton examen.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.invitations_arrive_in_your_requests_inbox_review_their_verified_profil": "Les invitations arrivent dans ta boîte de demandes. Examine leur profil vérifié, leurs photos et leur historique. Toi, tu décides de qui tu veux rencontrer.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.when_you_accept_an_invite_your_telegram_handle_is_shared_with_your_mat": "Quand tu acceptes une invitation, ton identifiant Telegram est partagé avec ton match pour discuter facilement là où vous passez déjà votre temps.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.why_are_mens_profiles_hidden_by_default": "Pourquoi les profils des hommes sont-ils masqués par défaut ?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.why_do_matches_move_to_telegram": "Pourquoi les correspondances passent-elles sur Telegram ?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.how_does_selfie_verification_work": "Comment fonctionne la vérification par selfie ?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.what_is_our_policy_on_sugar_dating_escorting": "Quelle est notre politique sur les relations sugar et l'escort ?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_follows_a_private_luxury_model_many_successful_ambitious_men_val": "Amara suit un modèle de luxe privé. Beaucoup d'hommes réussis et ambitieux valorisent la discrétion. En masquant les profils masculins du fil public, ton identité reste privée jusqu'à ce que tu choisisses d'inviter quelqu'un.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.we_believe_dating_should_lead_to_real_life_meetings_not_endless_in_app": "Nous pensons que les rencontres doivent mener à des rendez-vous dans la vraie vie, et non à des messages interminables dans l'app. Une fois l'intérêt mutuel confirmé, l'échange des identifiants Telegram permet une communication pratique, chiffrée et directe.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.every_member_must_complete_a_real_time_selfie_verification_during_onbo": "Chaque membre doit effectuer une vérification de selfie en temps réel lors de l'inscription. Cela garantit que chaque profil appartient à une personne authentique et vivante, et élimine les faux profils.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_strictly_prohibits_compensated_dating_sugar_arrangements_prostit": "Amara interdit strictement les rencontres rémunérées, les arrangements sugar, la prostitution ou tout échange financier entre membres. Les comptes violant ces règles sont bannis définitivement.",

  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.welcome_to_amara_we_respect_your_privacy_and_are_committed_to_protecti": "Bienvenue chez Amara. Nous respectons ta vie privée et nous nous engageons à protéger tes données personnelles. Cette politique de confidentialité t'informe de la manière dont nous veillons sur tes données lorsque tu visites notre application et t'énonce tes droits en matière de confidentialité ainsi que la protection que la loi t'accorde.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.personal_data_means_any_information_about_an_individual_from_which_tha": "Les données personnelles désignent toute information permettant d'identifier une personne. •",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.identity_data": "Données d'identité :",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.includes_username_marital_status_title_date_of_birth_and_gender": "comprennent le nom d'utilisateur, la situation matrimoniale, le titre, la date de naissance et le genre. •",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.contact_data": "Données de contact :",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.includes_email_address_and_telephone_numbers": "comprennent l'adresse e-mail et les numéros de téléphone. •",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.technical_data": "Données techniques :",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.includes_ip_address_login_data_device_info": "comprennent l'adresse IP, les données de connexion et les informations sur l'appareil. •",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.profile_data": "Données de profil :",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.includes_interests_preferences_and_photos": "comprennent les centres d'intérêt, les préférences et les photos.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.we_use_your_data_mainly_to_provide_you_with_matching_partner_recommend": "Nous utilisons tes données principalement pour te proposer des recommandations de partenaires compatibles et pour t'enregistrer en tant que nouvel utilisateur.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.we_retain_your_personal_data_only_as_long_as_necessary_to_fulfil_the_p": "Nous conservons tes données personnelles uniquement le temps nécessaire pour remplir les finalités pour lesquelles nous les avons collectées.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.by_accessing_the_amara_application_you_are_agreeing_to_be_bound_by_the": "En accédant à l'application Amara, tu acceptes d'être lié par ces conditions de service et toutes les lois applicables.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.permission_is_granted_to_use_materials_on_amaras_app_for_personal_non": "L'autorisation est accordée d'utiliser les contenus de l'app Amara uniquement pour une vision personnelle, non commerciale et temporaire.",
  "profile.privacypolicyscreen.always_exercise_caution_when_meeting_new_people_follow_our_safety_guid": "Fais toujours preuve de prudence quand tu rencontres de nouvelles personnes. Suis nos consignes de sécurité en dehors de l'app.",

  "profile.howtousescreen.dating_built_for_real_life": "Întâlniri create pentru viața reală.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_endless_swiping_no_spam_just_verified_singles_connecting_for_real_d": "Fără swipe-uri infinite. Fără spam. Doar persoane singure verificate care se conectează pentru întâlniri reale.",

  "profile.howtousescreen.100_private_profile": "Profil 100% privat",
  "profile.howtousescreen.send_date_invitations": "Trimite invitații de întâlnire",
  "profile.howtousescreen.review_acceptance": "Verificare & acceptare",

  "profile.howtousescreen.100_free_for_women": "100% gratuit pentru femei",
  "profile.howtousescreen.zero_spam_or_creepy_dms": "Zero spam sau mesaje ciudate",
  "profile.howtousescreen.you_are_always_in_control": "Tu ai mereu controlul",
  "profile.howtousescreen.chat_directly_on_telegram": "Discută direct pe Telegram",
  "profile.howtousescreen.your_profile_is_completely_hidden_from_the_public_feed_women_cannot_se": "Profilul tău este complet ascuns din feed-ul public. Femeile nu te pot căuta sau vedea fotografiile decât dacă alegi mai întâi să le trimiți o invitație.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.browse_verified_female_profiles_nearby_or_worldwide_find_someone_you_l": "Răsfoiește profiluri feminine verificate din apropiere sau din toată lumea. Găsește pe cineva pe care îți place și trimite o invitație directă de întâlnire (abonament necesar).",
  "profile.howtousescreen.when_the_woman_receives_your_invitation_she_reviews_your_profile_photo": "Când femeia primește invitația ta, ea îți verifică profilul, fotografiile și propunerea de întâlnire. Doar ea decide să accepte sau să refuze.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_endless_in_app_small_talk_once_she_accepts_both_of_your_telegram_ha": "Fără discuții interminabile în aplicație! Când acceptă, utilizatorii voștri de Telegram sunt deblocați în siguranță, astfel încât puteți discuta și planifica întâlnirea direct.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_is_completely_free_for_all_female_members_you_will_never_be_aske": "Amara este complet gratuit pentru toate membrele. Nu ți se va cere niciodată să plătești pentru invitații, abonament sau discuții.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.say_goodbye_to_hundreds_of_unwanted_messages_men_cannot_message_you_di": "Spune adio sutelor de mesaje nedorite. Bărbații nu îți pot scrie direct; pot trimite doar o invitație formală de întâlnire pe care să o verifici.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.invitations_arrive_in_your_requests_inbox_review_their_verified_profil": "Invitațiile ajung în Inboxul de solicitări. Verifică profilul lor verificat, fotografiile și istoricul. Tu decizi pe cine vrei să întâlnești.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.when_you_accept_an_invite_your_telegram_handle_is_shared_with_your_mat": "Când accepți o invitație, utilizatorul tău de Telegram este partajat cu match-ul tău, astfel încât puteți discuta convenabil acolo unde oricum vă petreceți timpul.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.why_are_mens_profiles_hidden_by_default": "De ce sunt profilele bărbaților ascuse implicit?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.why_do_matches_move_to_telegram": "De ce se mută potrivirile pe Telegram?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.how_does_selfie_verification_work": "Cum funcționează verificarea prin selfie?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.what_is_our_policy_on_sugar_dating_escorting": "Care este politica noastră privind întâlnirile de tip sugar și escorta?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_follows_a_private_luxury_model_many_successful_ambitious_men_val": "Amara urmează un model privat de lux. Mulți bărbați de succes și ambițioși apreciază discreția. Ascunzând profilele bărbaților din feed-ul public, identitatea ta rămâne privată până când alegi să inviți pe cineva.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.we_believe_dating_should_lead_to_real_life_meetings_not_endless_in_app": "Credem că întâlnirile ar trebui să ducă la întâlniri în viața reală, nu la mesaje interminabile în aplicație. Odată ce interesul mutual este confirmat, schimbul de utilizatori Telegram permite o comunicare convenabilă, criptată și directă.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.every_member_must_complete_a_real_time_selfie_verification_during_onbo": "Fiecare membru trebuie să completeze o verificare de selfie în timp real la înregistrare. Acest lucru garantează că fiecare profil aparține unei persoane reale și elimină profilele false.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_strictly_prohibits_compensated_dating_sugar_arrangements_prostit": "Amara interzice strict întâlnirile plătite, aranjamentele de tip sugar, prostituția sau orice schimb financiar între membri. Conturile care încalcă aceste standarde sunt blocate permanent.",

  "profile.howtousescreen.how_amara_works": "Как работает Amara",
  "profile.howtousescreen.exclusive_dating_experience": "ЭКСКЛЮЗИВНЫЙ ОПЫТ ЗНАКОМСТВ",
  "profile.howtousescreen.dating_built_for_real_life": "Знакомства для реальной жизни.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_endless_swiping_no_spam_just_verified_singles_connecting_for_real_d": "Бесконечных свайпов. Никакого спама. Только проверенные одинокие люди для реальных свиданий.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.for_men": "Для мужчин",
  "profile.howtousescreen.for_women": "Для женщин",
  "profile.howtousescreen.our_community_standards": "Наши стандарты сообщества",
  "profile.howtousescreen.photo_verified": "Фото подтверждено:",
  "profile.howtousescreen.every_profile_completes_a_live_gesture_selfie": "Каждый профиль проходит живую проверку селфи с жестом.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_commercial_dating": "Без коммерческих знакомств:",
  "profile.howtousescreen.escorts_prostitution_and_sugar_dating_are_strictly_prohibited": "Эскорт, проституция и «сахарные» отношения строго запрещены.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.high_quality": "Высокое качество:",
  "profile.howtousescreen.profiles_with_low_photo_quality_or_abusive_behavior_are_removed": "Профили с низким качеством фото или оскорбительным поведением удаляются.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.frequently_asked_questions": "Часто задаваемые вопросы",
  "profile.howtousescreen.common_questions_about_using_the_amara_dating_platform": "Частые вопросы об использовании платформы знакомств Amara.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.100_private_profile": "100% приватный профиль",
  "profile.howtousescreen.send_date_invitations": "Отправляй приглашения на свидания",
  "profile.howtousescreen.review_acceptance": "Проверка и принятие",
  "profile.howtousescreen.connect_on_telegram": "Подключиться в Telegram",
  "profile.howtousescreen.100_free_for_women": "100% бесплатно для женщин",
  "profile.howtousescreen.zero_spam_or_creepy_dms": "Ноль спама и навязчивых личных сообщений",
  "profile.howtousescreen.you_are_always_in_control": "Ты всегда всё контролируешь",
  "profile.howtousescreen.chat_directly_on_telegram": "Общайся прямо в Telegram",
  "profile.howtousescreen.your_profile_is_completely_hidden_from_the_public_feed_women_cannot_se": "Твой профиль полностью скрыт из общего списка. Женщины не могут искать тебя или видеть твои фотографии, пока ты не отправишь им приглашение.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.browse_verified_female_profiles_nearby_or_worldwide_find_someone_you_l": "Просматривай проверенные женские профили рядом или по всему миру. Найди понравившуюся и отправь прямое приглашение на свидание (нужна подписка).",
  "profile.howtousescreen.when_the_woman_receives_your_invitation_she_reviews_your_profile_photo": "Когда женщина получает твоё приглашение, она просматривает твой профиль, фотографии и предложение о свидании. Только она решает принять или отклонить.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.no_endless_in_app_small_talk_once_she_accepts_both_of_your_telegram_ha": "Бесконечных болтовень в приложении! Как только она согласится, ваши имена пользователей Telegram будут надёжно разблокированы, чтобы вы могли общаться и планировать свидание.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_is_completely_free_for_all_female_members_you_will_never_be_aske": "Amara полностью бесплатна для всех женщин. Тебя никогда не попросят платить за приглашения, подписку или общение.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.say_goodbye_to_hundreds_of_unwanted_messages_men_cannot_message_you_di": "Забудь о сотнях нежелательных сообщений. Мужчины не могут писать тебе напрямую; они могут только отправить официальное приглашение на свидание, которое ты рассмотришь.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.invitations_arrive_in_your_requests_inbox_review_their_verified_profil": "Приглашения появляются во входящих запросов. Просмотри проверенный профиль, фотографии и историю. Ты решаешь, с кем хочешь встретиться.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.when_you_accept_an_invite_your_telegram_handle_is_shared_with_your_mat": "Когда ты принимаешь приглашение, твоё имя пользователя Telegram передаётся совпадению, чтобы вы могли удобно общаться там, где и так проводите время.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.why_are_mens_profiles_hidden_by_default": "Почему профили мужчин скрыты по умолчанию?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.why_do_matches_move_to_telegram": "Почему совпадения переходят в Telegram?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.how_does_selfie_verification_work": "Как работает проверка селфи?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.what_is_our_policy_on_sugar_dating_escorting": "Какова ваша политика в отношении «сахарных» знакомств и эскорта?",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_follows_a_private_luxury_model_many_successful_ambitious_men_val": "Amara следует приватной модели люкс. Многие успешные и амбициозные мужчины ценят конфиденциальность. Скрывая мужские профили из общего списка, твоя личность остаётся приватной, пока ты не решишь кого-то пригласить.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.we_believe_dating_should_lead_to_real_life_meetings_not_endless_in_app": "Мы верим, что знакомства должны приводить к встречам в реальной жизни, а не к бесконечной переписке в приложении. Когда взаимный интерес подтверждён, обмен именами Telegram позволяет общаться удобно, безопасно и напрямую.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.every_member_must_complete_a_real_time_selfie_verification_during_onbo": "Каждый участник должен пройти живую проверку селфи при регистрации. Это гарантирует, что каждый профиль принадлежит настоящему человеку, и исключает фейковые аккаунты.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.amara_strictly_prohibits_compensated_dating_sugar_arrangements_prostit": "Amara строго запрещает платные знакомства, «сахарные» договорённости, проституцию или любой финансовый обмен между участниками. Аккаунты, нарушающие эти правила, блокируются навсегда.",
  "profile.howtousescreen.privacy_first": "КОНФИДЕНЦИАЛЬНОСТЬ ПРЕВЫШЕ ВСЕГО",
  "profile.howtousescreen.invitation_only": "ТОЛЬКО ПО ПРИГЛАШЕНИЮ",
